Cities

Each city page lists only locations inside that city's limits that were checked on an official store page or the official USPS location page. Counts are checked locations, not every place in the city.

Huntsville, added Nov 13, 2026, is the first city outside Colorado. It lists six Post Offices and five Walmart Supercenters inside Huntsville city limits (Madison County). The Walmart Supercenter pages show only a general "Money Services" label, so those stores are on the city hub without services. No Huntsville location names check cashing. Two Walmart Supercenters that print Madison or Owens Cross Roads addresses and two Post Offices (Redstone Arsenal and Normal) are held for review and not listed. Stores and Post Offices in Madison, Owens Cross Roads, Harvest, Meridianville, and other nearby places are not listed under Huntsville. Hours on the Huntsville pages are local Central Time.

Mobile, added Nov 13, 2026, lists twelve Post Offices and three Walmart Supercenters inside Mobile city limits (Mobile County). The Walmart Supercenter pages show only a general "Money Services" label, so those stores are on the city hub without services. No Mobile location names check cashing. The Prichard and Theodore Post Offices and the Walmart Supercenter at 101 E I65 Service Rd S are held for review and not listed. Stores and Post Offices in Prichard, Chickasaw, Saraland, Semmes, Theodore, and other nearby places are not listed under Mobile. Hours on the Mobile pages are local Central Time.

Anchorage, added Nov 14, 2026, is the first Alaska city. It lists ten Post Offices inside Anchorage municipality limits (the consolidated city-borough). No Anchorage location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Four Post Offices whose official pages print JBER, Eagle River, Chugiak, or Girdwood are held for review and not listed, even though the Census geocoder places them inside the municipality. Post Offices in Wasilla, Palmer, Houston, Seward, and other nearby places are not listed under Anchorage. Hours on the Anchorage pages are local Alaska Time.

Juneau, added Nov 14, 2026, is the second Alaska city. It lists three Post Offices inside Juneau city and borough limits (the consolidated city-borough). No Juneau location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Douglas Post Office is held for review and not listed, because its official page prints Douglas even though the Census geocoder places it inside the city and borough. Post Offices in Sitka, Haines, Hoonah, Gustavus, Skagway, Petersburg, and other nearby places are not listed under Juneau. Hours on the Juneau pages are local Alaska Time.

Fairbanks, added Nov 14, 2026, is the third Alaska city. It lists one Post Office inside Fairbanks city limits (an incorporated city in Fairbanks North Star Borough, not a consolidated city-borough). No Fairbanks location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Fairbanks Main Office and the College Post Office are held for review and not listed, because the Census geocoder places them in the College census-designated place, outside the city, even though their official pages print Fairbanks. The Fort Wainwright Post Office is held because its official page prints Fort Wainwright even though the geocoder places it inside the city. Post Offices in North Pole, Eielson AFB, Ester, Salcha, Nenana, and other nearby places are not listed under Fairbanks. Hours on the Fairbanks pages are local Alaska Time.

Wasilla, added Nov 14, 2026, is the fourth Alaska city. It lists one Post Office inside Wasilla city limits (an incorporated city in Matanuska-Susitna Borough, not a consolidated city-borough). No Wasilla location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Wasilla Annex is held for review and not listed, because the Census geocoder places its official map point in the South Lakes census-designated place, outside the city, even though its official page prints Wasilla; that page also lists no retail hours and no money orders. Post Offices in Houston, Palmer, Willow, Sutton, Talkeetna, and other nearby places are not listed under Wasilla. Hours on the Wasilla pages are local Alaska Time.

Sitka, added Nov 14, 2026, is the fifth Alaska city. It lists one Post Office inside Sitka city and borough limits (the consolidated city-borough). No Sitka location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Sitka Post Office closes at 5 p.m. on weekdays and is closed on weekends, so it is not labeled Open evenings or Open weekends, and Sitka has no evenings-and-weekends page (Check Desk does not publish an empty list). The Port Alexander Post Office is not listed under Sitka; it is a separate incorporated city. Hours on the Sitka pages are local Alaska Time.

Phoenix, added Nov 14, 2026, is the first Arizona city. It lists sixteen Post Offices inside Phoenix city limits (an incorporated city in Maricopa County, not a consolidated city-county). No Phoenix location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Laveen Post Office and the Laveen Annex are held for review and not listed, because their official pages print Laveen even though the Census geocoder places the addresses inside Phoenix city. Five other Phoenix-printed offices inside the city (Northwest, Sunnyslope, the Northwest Annex, and two processing plants) are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Post Offices in Glendale, Scottsdale, Tempe, Mesa, Chandler, Gilbert, Peoria, and other nearby places are not listed under Phoenix. Hours on the Phoenix pages are local Arizona Time (Mountain Standard Time; Arizona does not observe daylight saving).

Tucson, added Nov 14, 2026, is the second Arizona city. It lists eight Post Offices inside Tucson city limits (an incorporated city in Pima County, not a consolidated city-county). No Tucson location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Five Post Offices that print Tucson are held for review and not listed, because the Census geocoder places them outside the city (in South Tucson city, Oro Valley town, the Casas Adobes census-designated place, or the Drexel Heights census-designated place). The Vail Annex is held because it prints Vail while its current official map point falls inside Tucson city. Four other Tucson-printed offices inside the city, including the Cherrybell Post Office, are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Post Offices in Marana, Sahuarita, Green Valley, Vail, Catalina, and other nearby places are not listed under Tucson. Hours on the Tucson pages are local Arizona Time (Mountain Standard Time; Arizona does not observe daylight saving).

Mesa, added Nov 14, 2026, is the third Arizona city. It lists eight Post Offices inside Mesa city limits (an incorporated city in Maricopa County, not a consolidated city-county). No Mesa location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. No Mesa-printed office was held, and no in-city Mesa office without money-order tags was found. Post Offices in Phoenix, Gilbert, Chandler, Tempe, Scottsdale, Apache Junction, Queen Creek, Higley, and other nearby places are not listed under Mesa. Hours on the Mesa pages are local Arizona Time (Mountain Standard Time; Arizona does not observe daylight saving).

Gilbert, added Nov 14, 2026, is the fourth Arizona city. It lists two Post Offices inside Gilbert town limits (legally a town, an incorporated place in Maricopa County, not a consolidated city-county). No Gilbert location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Higley Post Office is held for review and not listed, because its official page prints Higley while the official map point falls inside Gilbert town. Two other Gilbert-printed offices inside the town (the Fiesta Annex and the Gilbert Roadrunner Annex) are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Post Offices in Mesa, Chandler, Phoenix, Tempe, Queen Creek, and other nearby places are not listed under Gilbert. Hours on the Gilbert pages are local Arizona Time (Mountain Standard Time; Arizona does not observe daylight saving).

Chandler, added Nov 14, 2026, is the fifth Arizona city. It lists three Post Offices inside Chandler city limits (an incorporated city in Maricopa County, not a consolidated city-county). No Chandler location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. No Chandler-printed office was held, and no in-city Chandler office without money-order tags was found. The Ocotillo Post Office is in ZIP 85248, which is also an official Sun Lakes ZIP, but its address and official map point are inside Chandler city, not the Sun Lakes census-designated place. Post Offices in Phoenix, Mesa, Gilbert, Tempe, Queen Creek, Sun Lakes, and other nearby places are not listed under Chandler. Hours on the Chandler pages are local Arizona Time (Mountain Standard Time; Arizona does not observe daylight saving).

Little Rock, added Nov 15, 2026, is the first Arkansas city. It lists ten Post Offices inside Little Rock city limits (an incorporated city in Pulaski County, not a consolidated city-county). North Little Rock is a separate incorporated city and is not listed here. No Little Rock location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The GMF Box Section is held for review and not listed, because it prints Little Rock while the Census geocoder places it in North Little Rock city. The Mabelvale and Alexander Post Offices are held because they print other cities while their addresses fall inside Little Rock city. Two other Little Rock-printed offices inside the city, including the Little Rock Post Office at 600 E Capitol Ave, are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Post Offices in North Little Rock, Sherwood, Jacksonville, Maumelle, Bryant, Benton, and other nearby places are not listed under Little Rock. Hours on the Little Rock pages are local Central Time.

Fayetteville, added Nov 15, 2026, is the second Arkansas city. It lists two Post Offices inside Fayetteville city limits (an incorporated city in Washington County, not a consolidated city-county). No Fayetteville location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The Greenland Post Office is held for review and not listed, because its official page prints Greenland while the official map point falls inside Fayetteville city. Post Offices in Springdale, Farmington, Tontitown, Rogers, Bentonville, and other nearby places are not listed under Fayetteville. Hours on the Fayetteville pages are local Central Time.

Fort Smith, added Nov 15, 2026, is the third Arkansas city. It lists four Post Offices inside Fort Smith city limits (an incorporated city in Sebastian County, not a consolidated city-county), including the GMF Fort Smith Post Office, which has Saturday hours and a weekday close after 5 p.m. No Fort Smith location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Two other Fort Smith-printed records at the same 3318 S 74th St address as the GMF office are not listed, because their official pages do not list money orders and do not publish a BUSINESS hours block. Post Offices in Van Buren, Barling, Greenwood, Alma, Arkoma, and other nearby Arkansas and Oklahoma places are not listed under Fort Smith. Hours on the Fort Smith pages are local Central Time.

Springdale, added Nov 15, 2026, is the fourth Arkansas city. It lists two Post Offices inside Springdale city limits. Springdale is one incorporated Census place spanning Washington and Benton counties, not a consolidated city-county; both listed offices are in the Washington County part of that place. No Springdale location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Post Offices in Fayetteville, Tontitown, Elm Springs, Lowell, Rogers, Bentonville, and other nearby places are not listed under Springdale. Hours on the Springdale pages are local Central Time.

Jonesboro, added Nov 15, 2026, is the fifth Arkansas city. It lists three Post Offices inside Jonesboro city limits (an incorporated city in Craighead County, not a consolidated city-county). No Jonesboro location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. The State University Post Office is held for review and not listed, because its official page prints State University while the address and official map point fall inside Jonesboro city. Three other Jonesboro-printed records, including the Jonesboro Post Office at 2404 Race St and two records at 3405 Moore Rd, are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Post Offices in Brookland, Bono, Bay, Paragould, Trumann, and other nearby places are not listed under Jonesboro. Hours on the Jonesboro pages are local Central Time.

Los Angeles, added Nov 15, 2026, is the first California city. It lists 43 Post Offices inside Los Angeles city limits (an incorporated city in Los Angeles County, not a consolidated city-county). Long Beach, Beverly Hills, Santa Monica, West Hollywood, Culver City, Burbank, Pasadena, Inglewood, and other nearby places are separate incorporated cities or communities and are not listed here. Neighborhood postal cities such as Van Nuys, San Pedro, North Hollywood, and many San Fernando Valley and harbor communities are held for review when the official page prints that neighborhood name, even if the address and official map point fall inside Los Angeles city. The Hollywood Post Office on Wilcox Avenue prints Los Angeles, so it is listed. Five Los Angeles-printed offices are held because the Census geocoder does not place both the address and the official map point fully inside Los Angeles city, including offices in East Los Angeles CDP and points in Inglewood city. Ten other Los Angeles-printed records inside the city are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. No Los Angeles location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the Los Angeles pages are local Pacific Time.

San Diego, added Nov 15, 2026, is the second California city. It lists 27 Post Offices inside San Diego city limits (an incorporated city in San Diego County, not a consolidated city-county). Chula Vista, National City, Coronado, Imperial Beach, La Mesa, El Cajon, Santee, Poway, and other nearby places are separate incorporated cities or communities and are not listed here. The Ocean Beach and Pacific Beach offices print San Diego, so they are listed. Offices that print La Jolla or San Ysidro are held for review, even though their addresses and official map points fall inside San Diego city. The NAS North Island Post Office is held because it prints San Diego while the official map point falls in Coronado city. Eleven other San Diego-printed records inside the city are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Only Carmel Mountain Postal Store publishes a weekday close after 5 p.m. No San Diego location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the San Diego pages are local Pacific Time.

San Jose, added Nov 15, 2026, is the third California city. It lists nine Post Offices inside San Jose city limits (an incorporated city in Santa Clara County, not a consolidated city-county). Santa Clara, Sunnyvale, Milpitas, Campbell, Cupertino, Los Gatos, and other nearby places are separate incorporated cities or communities and are not listed here. The Willow Glen Post Office prints San Jose, so it is listed. The Alviso Post Office is held for review because it prints Alviso while the address and official map point fall inside San Jose city. The New Almaden Post Office is held because it prints San Jose while the address and official map point fall in unincorporated Santa Clara County. Nine other San Jose-printed records inside the city are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders. Only Willow Glen publishes a weekday close after 5 p.m. No San Jose location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the San Jose pages are local Pacific Time.

San Francisco, added Nov 15, 2026, is the fourth California city. It lists 31 Post Offices inside San Francisco city limits (a consolidated city-county, coextensive with San Francisco County). South San Francisco is a separate incorporated city in San Mateo County and is not listed here. Daly City, Oakland, Berkeley, San Bruno, and other nearby places are also separate incorporated cities or communities and are not listed here. Five San Francisco-printed offices are listed under a documented map-point fallback, because one or both Census address files did not match after street-name retries while the official USPS map point falls inside San Francisco city: Diamond Heights, Lakeshore Plaza, Marina, Bryant Street, and Presidio. No San Francisco-printed office was held for sitting outside the city, and no office that prints another city was held for sitting inside it. Nine other San Francisco-printed records inside the city are not listed because their official pages do not list money orders, including the West Portal Post Office, which publishes Saturday hours. Seven listed offices publish a weekday close after 5 p.m., including the San Francisco P&DC Finance Post Office, which closes at 8:30 p.m. on weekdays. No San Francisco location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the San Francisco pages are local Pacific Time.

Fresno, added Nov 15, 2026, is the fifth California city. It lists eight Post Offices inside Fresno city limits (an incorporated city in Fresno County, not a consolidated city-county). Clovis, Fowler, Sanger, Selma, Kerman, and other nearby places are separate incorporated cities or communities and are not listed here. The Pinedale Post Office prints Fresno, so it is listed. The Clinter Post Office is listed under a documented map-point fallback, because the current Census address file did not match after street-name retries while the official USPS map point falls inside Fresno city. The Barton and Calwa Post Offices are held because they print Fresno while the address and official map point fall in Calwa CDP, not inside Fresno city. One other Fresno-printed record inside the city, Civic Center Fresno, is not listed because its official page does not list money orders. None of the eight listed offices publishes a weekday close after 5 p.m. No Fresno location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the Fresno pages are local Pacific Time.

The nearby-city lists now include Post Offices, King Soopers stores, and Walmart Supercenters checked on their official pages on Nov 11, 2026. "None listed" under check cashing means no checked location in that city names check cashing on its official page. The new Walmart Supercenter pages in Aurora, Commerce City, Englewood, and Lakewood show only a general "Money Services" label, so those stores are on the city hubs without services and are not counted for money orders or check cashing; call the store. Wheat Ridge and Federal Heights have no Walmart Supercenter inside city limits, and Walmart Neighborhood Markets are not listed. City pages list a location under the city whose limits it is in, even when its mailing address names a different city, and places outside these city limits (such as unincorporated Adams County) are not listed.

Colorado Springs, added Nov 11, 2026, lists Post Offices, King Soopers stores, and Walmart Supercenters inside Colorado Springs city limits. Its Walmart Supercenter pages also show only a general "Money Services" label, so those stores are on the city hub without services. Stores and Post Offices with a Colorado Springs mailing address that are outside city limits (for example in Monument, Fountain, Cimarron Hills, or Security-Widefield) are not listed.

Fort Collins, added Nov 11, 2026, lists Post Offices, King Soopers stores, and Walmart Supercenters inside Fort Collins city limits. Its two Walmart Supercenter pages also show only a general "Money Services" label, so those stores are on the city hub without services. Stores in nearby towns such as Timnath, Windsor, and Loveland are not listed.

Thornton, added Nov 11, 2026, lists King Soopers stores and a Walmart Supercenter inside Thornton city limits. Its Walmart Supercenter page also shows only a general "Money Services" label, so that store is on the city hub without services. No Thornton store page names check cashing, and no Post Office is listed yet. Stores in Northglenn, Westminster, Brighton, and other nearby cities are not listed under Thornton.

Arvada, added Nov 11, 2026, lists Post Offices, King Soopers stores, and a Walmart Supercenter inside Arvada city limits. Its Walmart Supercenter page and one King Soopers page (Candelas) show only a general "Money Services" label, so those stores are on the city hub without services. No Arvada store page names check cashing. Stores in Westminster, Wheat Ridge, Lakeside, Golden, and other nearby places are not listed under Arvada.

Urban Honolulu, added Nov 16, 2026, is the first Hawaii community in a one-time exception that ships Hawaii's largest Census communities (CDPs). It lists ten Post Offices inside the Urban Honolulu Census Designated Place (GEOID 1571550) in Honolulu County. Urban Honolulu is a Census community, not an incorporated city; Hawaii has no incorporated places recognized by the Census Bureau. The official printed city is Honolulu. This is Urban Honolulu, Hawaii, not a Honolulu in another state, and not East Honolulu, Pearl City, or Waipahu. All ten listed offices sell money orders: Ala Moana, Downtown Honolulu, Honolulu, Kaimuki, Kapalama, Makiki, Moiliili, Uptown, Waialae Kahala, and Waikiki. One is labeled Open evenings: the Honolulu Post Office, until 8:00 p.m. Six publish Saturday hours and are labeled Open weekends: Ala Moana, Honolulu, Kapalama, Makiki, Waialae Kahala, and Waikiki. None publishes Sunday hours. Ala Moana is listed on a documented mixed-vintage fallback: the current Census address file matched in Urban Honolulu CDP, the 2020 file did not match after street and ZIP retries, and both official map points fall inside the CDP. Makiki is listed on a documented map-point fallback: neither address file matched 1111 Lunalilo St after street, city, and ZIP retries (no-ZIP hits snapped to Lunalilo Home Road in East Honolulu and were discarded), and both official map points fall inside Urban Honolulu CDP. Waialae Kahala geocodes in Urban Honolulu, not East Honolulu. Aina Haina and Hawaii Kai print Honolulu but geocode in the East Honolulu CDP, so they are held out of this list. Fort Shafter and Tripler Army Medical Center sit in Urban Honolulu CDP but print Fort Shafter and Tripler Army Medical Center, so they are held. Sand Island Carrier Annex prints Honolulu and geocodes in the CDP, but its official page does not list money orders, so it is not listed. No Urban Honolulu location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the Urban Honolulu pages are Hawaii Time (HST).

This is Kansas City, Kansas, in Wyandotte County, not Kansas City, Missouri, and not Bonner Springs, Edwardsville, or Lake Quivira. The pages live at /kansas-city-ks/ so they will not collide with Kansas City, Missouri later. Offices that print Kansas City, Missouri, are not on this list. All five listed offices sell money orders. None is labeled Open evenings; weekday retail closes at 5:00 p.m. or earlier. Four publish Saturday hours, 9:00 a.m. to 12:00 p.m., and are labeled Open weekends: Civic Center, Robert L Roberts, Rosedale, and Wyandotte West. None publishes Sunday hours. Argentine is closed on Saturday and Sunday and closes at 12:00 p.m. on weekdays, so it is not on the evenings-and-weekends list. A 5:00 p.m. close does not count as Open evenings. All five match the current and 2020 Census address files in Kansas City city, Kansas, and both official map points fall there. No map-point fallback was used. Two other offices print Kansas City, Kansas, and geocode in the city, but their official pages do not list money orders, so they are not listed: the Kansas City Post Office at 550 Nebraska Ave (the same printed street address as the listed Civic Center office), and the Muncie Post Office at 6540 Kaw Dr. No other office in the Kansas locator sweep geocodes into Kansas City, Kansas, including the 23 offices that print Kansas City, Missouri. No Kansas City, Kansas location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the Kansas City, Kansas pages are Central Time.

Louisville, added Nov 16, 2026, is the first Kentucky city. It lists eight Post Offices inside Louisville, Kentucky, city limits (the Census place Louisville/Jefferson County metro government (balance), GEOID 2148006, in Jefferson County, including the former Louisville city extent): the Downtown Louisville, Dr Martin Luther King Jr, Hikes Point, Iroquois, Okolona, Pleasure Ridge Park, Shelby, and Shively Post Offices. This is Louisville, Kentucky, in Jefferson County, not Louisville, Colorado (already on Check Desk at /louisville/), and not Jeffersontown, St. Matthews, Shively city, Lyndon, Middletown, Anchorage, or the other remaining Jefferson County municipalities. City-limits checks use the SUMLEV 162 balance, not the county or SUMLEV 170 total. The Census geocoder still splits that balance into a non-functioning former-city polygon (GEOID 2148000) and the remainder (GEOID 2148006); ACS and the Population Estimates Program include the former city in the balance, so offices in either polygon are listed here. The Shively Post Office at 3131 Crums Ln prints Louisville and geocodes in the balance, not in Shively city, so it is listed here. All eight listed offices sell money orders. One is labeled Open evenings: Okolona, until 6:00 p.m. Six publish Saturday hours and are labeled Open weekends. None publishes Sunday hours. The Downtown Louisville and Iroquois Post Offices are closed on Saturday and Sunday. A 5:00 p.m. close does not count as Open evenings. All eight match the current and 2020 Census address files, and both official map points fall inside the PEP/ACS balance. No map-point fallback was used. Six offices print Louisville but sit in other Jefferson County municipalities: Anchorage, Annshire Annex (in Watterson Park), Jeffersontown, Lyndon, Middletown, and Saint Matthews. Five offices geocode in the balance but print another city, so they are held out of this list: Eastwood, Fairdale, Fisherville, Harrods Creek, and Masonic Home. Eight other offices print Louisville and geocode in the balance, but their official pages do not list money orders, so they are not listed. No other office in the Kentucky locator sweep geocodes into this Louisville place except those printed-city holds. No Louisville, KY location names check cashing. Walmart Supercenters have not been checked yet, because the official walmart.com store locator was unavailable when this page was built. Hours on the Louisville, KY pages are Eastern Time.
